A simple RLVR recipe for BIRD: prompt and model selection (GEPA mentioned), offline RL warm-up, then online RLVR. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/ali2025sqlrlvr \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2509.21459) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21459)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rlvr\">rlvr</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/scaling\">scaling</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-data\">nl2dsl-data</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-select\">nl2dsl-select</a></span>", "paper:alrashed2026stochastic": "- **[The Stochastic Shift](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.21133 \"The Stochastic Shift: A New Evaluation Paradigm for Text-to-SQL with AI Operators (2026)\")**: A New Evaluation Paradigm for Text-to-SQL with AI Operators (preprint 2026). Evaluates text-to-SQL whose queries call AI operators, on BigQuery and ThalamusDB (abstract). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/alrashed2026stochastic \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.21133) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21133) · [Code](https://figshare.com/s/c75141469fb6dc74b890)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/judge\">judge</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-eval\">nl2dsl-eval</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nondet-eval\">nondet-eval</a></span>", "paper:asadolahi2026memoryinflation": "- **[Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.00017 \"Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents (2026)\")** ((Echo Gap, LUCID), preprint 2026). A memory agent stores each episode with a score it gives itself and reuses high-scored ones; the authors call the inflated scores of wrong episodes the \"Echo Gap\", argue that reuse compounds it, and state an Error-Independence Assumption (EIA): a correcting signal must track truth and have errors uncorrelated with the self-grade's (abstract; §1; §3). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/asadolahi2026memoryinflation \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.00017) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00017)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/hacking\">hacking</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/judge\">judge</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/nl\">nl</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl\">nl2dsl</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/promptopt-context\">promptopt-context</a></span>", "paper:bai2023querybooster": "- **[QueryBooster](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2305.08272 \"QueryBooster: Improving SQL Performance Using Middleware Services for Human-Centered Query Rewriting (2023)\")**: Improving SQL Performance Using Middleware Services for Human-Centered Query Rewriting (PVLDB 16(11) 2023). Human-centered rewriting middleware: users write rules (VarSQL) or give example pairs it generalizes. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/bai2023querybooster \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2305.08272) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.08272) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3611479.3611497) · [Code](https://github.com/ISG-ICS/QueryBooster)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite-classic\">rewrite-classic</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-discover\">rules-discover</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-lib\">rules-lib</a></span>", "paper:bai2026sqlrewriter": "- **[SqlRewriter](#/papers/bai2026sqlrewriter \"SqlRewriter: Harnessing Community Knowledge to Rewrite SQL Queries (2026)\")**: Harnessing Community Knowledge to Rewrite SQL Queries (ICDE 2026 (demo)). A community platform for contributing and sharing rewrite rules. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/bai2026sqlrewriter \"Summary\") · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1109/icde65706.2026.00326)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite-classic\">rewrite-classic</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-discover\">rules-discover</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-lib\">rules-lib</a></span>", "paper:begoli2018calcite": "- **[Apache Calcite](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1802.10233 \"Apache Calcite: A Foundational Framework for Optimized Query Processing Over Heterogeneous Data Sources (2018)\")**: A Foundational Framework for Optimized Query Processing Over Heterogeneous Data Sources (SIGMOD 2018). A framework with parsing, relational algebra, many optimization rules and a Volcano-style planner. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/begoli2018calcite \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1802.10233) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/1802.10233) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3183713.3190662) · [Code](https://github.com/apache/calcite)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/qo\">qo</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/pairgen-apply\">pairgen-apply</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite-classic\">rewrite-classic</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-lib\">rules-lib</a></span>", "paper:benzaken2019coq": "- **[A Coq mechanised formal semantics for realistic SQL queries: formally reconciling SQL and bag relational algebra](#/papers/benzaken2019coq \"A Coq mechanised formal semantics for realistic SQL queries: formally reconciling SQL and bag relational algebra (2019)\")** (CPP 2019). Executable Coq semantics for SELECT … GROUP BY … HAVING with NULLs, aggregates and correlated subqueries. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/benzaken2019coq \"Summary\") · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3293880.3294107)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/itp\">itp</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/theory\">theory</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a></span>", "paper:bhuiyan2024redossok": "- **[SoK: A Literature and Engineering Review of Regular Expression Denial of Service (ReDoS)](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2406.11618 \"SoK: A Literature and Engineering Review of Regular Expression Denial of Service (ReDoS) (2025)\")** (ASIA CCS 2025). A systematization of knowledge on ReDoS (regular-expression denial of service) in two parts: a literature review of 35 papers from security, software-engineering and programming-languages conferences, 2015–2024, sorted by research direction (detection, prevention, mitigation) and compared on threat model, definition of a vulnerability and evaluation method (§3, Fig. 4, Tabs. 2–4); and an engineering review of the regex engines of nine programming languages: which ReDoS defense each has, since when, at what code size, and whether it is on by default (§4.1, Tab. 5). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/bhuiyan2024redossok \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2406.11618) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11618) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3708821.3733912) · [Code](https://github.com/PurdueDualityLab/SoKReDoS-ASIACCS25)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/study\">study</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/survey\">survey</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-regex\">dsl-regex</a></span>", "paper:borthwick2026robophd": "- **[RoboPhD](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2601.01126 \"RoboPhD: Self-Improving Text-to-SQL Through Autonomous Agent Evolution (2026)\")**: Self-Improving Text-to-SQL Through Autonomous Agent Evolution (preprint 2026). An evolution agent rewrites a text-to-SQL agent (a database-analysis script and SQL-generation instructions) from performance feedback, with ELO-based selection (abstract). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/borthwick2026robophd \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2601.01126) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01126) · [Code](https://github.com/andborth/RoboPhD)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl\">nl2dsl</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/promptopt-context\">promptopt-context</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/promptopt-evolve\">promptopt-evolve</a></span>", "paper:chaudhuri1993real": "- **[Optimization of real conjunctive queries](#/papers/chaudhuri1993real \"Optimization of real conjunctive queries (1993)\")** (PODS 1993). Bag semantics for CQs (SQL without DISTINCT), which it says Dayal et al. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/chaudhuri1993real \"Summary\") · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/153850.153856)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/theory-bag\">theory-bag</a></span>", "paper:chen2019regel": "- **[Multi-modal Synthesis of Regular Expressions](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1908.03316 \"Multi-modal Synthesis of Regular Expressions (2020)\")** (PLDI 2020). Synthesizes a regular expression from an English description together with positive and negative example strings: a semantic parser turns the English into a ranked list of hierarchical sketches (partial regexes whose holes carry hints), and a programming-by-example engine completes each sketch into a regex consistent with the examples (abstract; §1). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/chen2019regel \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1908.03316) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/1908.03316) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3385412.3385988)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/labels\">labels</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl\">nl2dsl</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/smt\">smt</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-regex\">dsl-regex</a></span>", "paper:chen2025gbvsql": "- **[GBV-SQL](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2509.12612 \"GBV-SQL: Guided Generation and SQL2Text Back-Translation Validation for Multi-Agent Text2SQL (2025)\")**: Guided Generation and SQL2Text Back-Translation Validation for Multi-Agent Text2SQL (preprint 2025 (the litsearch listed ACL 2025)). Multi-agent text-to-SQL that translates the generated SQL back into text and checks it against the question. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/chen2025gbvsql \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2509.12612) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.12612)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/judge\">judge</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/study\">study</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-data\">nl2dsl-data</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-select\">nl2dsl-select</a></span>", "paper:chen2026equitrace": "- **[EquiTrace](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.30100 \"Building SQL Equivalence Proofs from Query Rewrite Rules (2026)\")**: Building SQL Equivalence Proofs from Query Rewrite Rules (preprint Oct 2026 (CIDR 2027 class file)). Proves two SQL queries equivalent by rewriting both, with admitted rewrite rules, to a common logical expression; the two derivations are the proof, returned as a replayable certificate, else UNKNOWN. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/chen2026equitrace \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.30100) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30100)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/qo\">qo</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-eval\">nl2dsl-eval</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/prove-memo\">prove-memo</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite-eval\">rewrite-eval</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-lib\">rules-lib</a></span>", "paper:chen2026lse": "- **[Learning to Self-Evolve (LSE)](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2603.18620 \"Learning to Self-Evolve (2026)\")**: Learning to Self-Evolve (preprint 2026). RL-trains a 4B model to edit its own context; each edit is rewarded by the downstream improvement. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/chen2026lse \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2603.18620) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18620) · [Code](https://github.com/chenyn66/learning-to-self-evolve)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rlvr\">rlvr</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-data\">nl2dsl-data</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/promptopt-evolve\">promptopt-evolve</a></span>", "paper:chu2016hottsql": "- **[HoTTSQL](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1607.04822 \"HoTTSQL: proving query rewrites with univalent SQL semantics (2017)\")**: proving query rewrites with univalent SQL semantics (PLDI 2017). Machine-checkable denotational SQL semantics in Coq: K-relations plus homotopy type theory, covering bags, correlated subqueries, aggregation. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/chu2016hottsql \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1607.04822) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/1607.04822) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3062341.3062348) · [Code](https://github.com/uwdb/Cosette)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/itp\">itp</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/prove\">prove</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-verify\">rules-verify</a></span>", "paper:chu2018udp": "- **[UDP](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1802.02229 \"Axiomatic foundations and algorithms for deciding semantic equivalences of SQL queries (2018)\")**: Axiomatic foundations and algorithms for deciding semantic equivalences of SQL queries (PVLDB 11(11) 2018). U-semirings: semiring semantics with unbounded summation and duplicate elimination, implemented in a proof assistant. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/chu2018udp \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1802.02229) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/1802.02229) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3236187.3236200) · [Code](https://github.com/uwdb/Cosette)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/itp\">itp</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/prove\">prove</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-verify\">rules-verify</a></span>", "paper:clark1999xpath": "- **[XML Path Language (XPath) Version 1.0](#/papers/clark1999xpath \"XML Path Language (XPath) Version 1.0 (1999)\")** (W3C Recommendation 16 November 1999 (status updated October 2016); a standard, filed as a paper). Defines XPath 1.0, \"a language for addressing parts of an XML document\" made for XSLT and XPointer (abstract; §1), with a grammar in EBNF (§1), a tree data model of seven node types (§5) and a core function library every implementation must support (§1; §4). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/clark1999xpath \"Summary\") · [Paper](https://www.w3.org/TR/1999/REC-xpath-19991116)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-path\">dsl-path</a></span>", "paper:coburn1993logic": "- **[A logic for rule-based query optimization in graph-based data models](https://link.springer.com/content/pdf/10.1007/3-540-57530-8_8.pdf \"A logic for rule-based query optimization in graph-based data models (1993)\")** (DOOD 1993 (LNCS 760)). A wide-spectrum algebra and refinement calculus for graph-based data models (abstract). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/coburn1993logic \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://link.springer.com/content/pdf/10.1007/3-540-57530-8_8.pdf) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1007/3-540-57530-8_8)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/qo\">qo</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nondet-semantics\">nondet-semantics</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite-classic\">rewrite-classic</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-lib\">rules-lib</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-verify\">rules-verify</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/theory-bag\">theory-bag</a></span>", "paper:cohen2007aggregate": "- **[Deciding Equivalences among Conjunctive Aggregate Queries](#/papers/cohen2007aggregate \"Deciding Equivalences among Conjunctive Aggregate Queries (2007)\")** (J. ACM 54(2) 2007). Equivalence characterizations for CQs with comparisons plus count, min/max or sum (no HAVING); for count-distinct a sufficient condition, also shown necessary in special cases (abstract; §5.1; Thm. 7.3, Cor. 7.4). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/cohen2007aggregate \"Summary\") · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/1219092.1219093)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/theory-bag\">theory-bag</a></span>", "paper:dharwada2025lithe": "- **[LITHE](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2502.12918 \"LITHE: A Query Rewrite Advisor using LLMs (2026)\")**: A Query Rewrite Advisor using LLMs (EDBT 2026). Prompt ensemble, database-sensitive prompts and LLM token-probability-guided rewrite paths (MCTS). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/dharwada2025lithe \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2502.12918) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12918) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.48786/edbt.2026.20)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite\">rewrite</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/scaling\">scaling</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a></span>", "paper:ding2023sqlsolver": "- **[SQLSolver](https://www.cs.yale.edu/homes/piskac/papers/2024SIGMOD.pdf \"Proving Query Equivalence Using Linear Integer Arithmetic (2023)\")**: Proving Query Equivalence Using Linear Integer Arithmetic (PACMMOD 1(4) / SIGMOD 2024, 2023). Unbounded prover that handles U-expression *unbounded summations* via LIA\\* (linear integer arithmetic with stars). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/ding2023sqlsolver \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://www.cs.yale.edu/homes/piskac/papers/2024SIGMOD.pdf) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3626768) · [Code](https://github.com/SJTU-IPADS/SQLSolver)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/prove\">prove</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/smt\">smt</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-discover\">rules-discover</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-verify\">rules-verify</a></span>", "paper:draper2010semantics": "- **[XQuery 1.0 and XPath 2.0 Formal Semantics (Second Edition)](#/papers/draper2010semantics \"XQuery 1.0 and XPath 2.0 Formal Semantics (Second Edition) (2010)\")** (W3C Recommendation 2010, revised 2015 (a standard, not a paper)). W3C's formal semantics of XQuery 1.0 and XPath 2.0: each expression is rewritten (\"normalized\") into a smaller Core language, which gets static typing rules and dynamic evaluation rules, written as inference rules (§3.2; §4). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/draper2010semantics \"Summary\") · [Paper](http://www.w3.org/TR/2010/REC-xquery-semantics-20101214/)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-path\">dsl-path</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nondet-semantics\">nondet-semantics</a></span>", "paper:fandinno2025anthem": "- **[anthem 2.0](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2507.11704 \"anthem 2.0: Automated Reasoning for Answer Set Programming (2025)\")**: Automated Reasoning for Answer Set Programming (Theory and Practice of Logic Programming 25(4) 2025 (ICLP 2025)). A tool that verifies answer set programs written in mini-gringo, a fragment of the clingo solver's input language with arithmetic and simple choice rules but no aggregates (abstract, §2): it proves strong equivalence (the same stable models inside any larger program), external equivalence (the same output for every input that a \"user guide\" permits) and adherence to a first-order specification (§4.1–4.3). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/fandinno2025anthem \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2507.11704) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11704) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1017/S1471068425100112) · [Code](https://github.com/potassco/anthem)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/prove\">prove</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-datalog\">dsl-datalog</a></span>", "paper:fathollahzadeh2026resequel": "- **[ReSequel](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.20853 \"ReSequel: Robust LLM-assisted Query Rewriting and Optimization using Templatization and Sampling (2026)\")**: Robust LLM-assisted Query Rewriting and Optimization using Templatization and Sampling (PVLDB 19(10), 2026). An outer optimization layer that rewrites query templates with LLMs. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/fathollahzadeh2026resequel \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.20853) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20853) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3828612.3828639) · [Code](https://github.com/CoDS-GCS/ReSequel)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite\">rewrite</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/scaling\">scaling</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a></span>", "paper:ganski1987nested": "- **[Optimization of Nested SQL Queries Revisited](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/38713.38723 \"Optimization of Nested SQL Queries Revisited (1987)\")** (SIGMOD 1987). Shows that Kim's unnesting algorithm NEST-JA is wrong for COUNT over empty groups (the \"COUNT bug\", found by Kiessling in a 1984 memo, §5.1) and for join predicates other than equality (§5.3); the outer-join fix for the first then fails when the outer relation has duplicates in the join column (§5.4). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/ganski1987nested \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/38713.38723) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/38713.38723)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/qo\">qo</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/study\">study</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite-classic\">rewrite-classic</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-lib\">rules-lib</a></span>", "paper:geneves2007efficient": "- **[Efficient Static Analysis of XML Paths and Types](https://inria.hal.science/inria-00502789/document \"Efficient Static Analysis of XML Paths and Types (2007)\")** (PLDI 2007). Decides XPath emptiness, containment, overlap and coverage, with or without regular tree type constraints (DTDs and other XML schemas), by testing whether one formula over finite trees is satisfiable (abstract, PDF p. 1; §8, PDF pp. 8–9); the XPath fragment \"covers all features except counting [8] and data values\" (§1, PDF p. 1), though its abstract syntax (Fig. 3, PDF p. 3) has 11 of XPath 1.0's 13 axes (no attribute or namespace axis). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/geneves2007efficient \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://inria.hal.science/inria-00502789/document) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/1250734.1250773)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/prove\">prove</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-path\">dsl-path</a></span>", "paper:graefe1993volcano": "- **[The Volcano Optimizer Generator](#/papers/graefe1993volcano \"The Volcano optimizer generator: extensibility and efficient search (1993)\")**: The Volcano optimizer generator: extensibility and efficient search (ICDE 1993). An optimizer generator: transformation and implementation rules in, a memoizing top-down search out. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/graefe1993volcano \"Summary\") · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1109/icde.1993.344061)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/qo\">qo</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-lib\">rules-lib</a></span>", "paper:graefe1995cascades": "- **[The Cascades Framework for Query Optimization](#/papers/graefe1995cascades \"The Cascades Framework for Query Optimization (1995)\")** (IEEE Data Eng. Bull. 18(3) 1995). Volcano's successor: rules and tasks as objects, a memo of groups, interleaved exploration and implementation. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/graefe1995cascades \"Summary\") · [Paper](http://sites.computer.org/debull/95SEP-CD.pdf)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/qo\">qo</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-lib\">rules-lib</a></span>", "paper:green2007provenance": "- **[Provenance Semirings](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/1265530.1265535 \"Provenance Semirings (2007)\")** (PODS 2007). Relations annotated with elements of a commutative semiring (K-relations): sets, bags, lineage and why-provenance are special cases (§2–4). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/green2007provenance \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/1265530.1265535) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/1265530.1265535)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/theory-bag\">theory-bag</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/theory-set\">theory-set</a></span>", "paper:guagliardo2017semantics": "- **[A Formal Semantics of SQL Queries](#/papers/guagliardo2017semantics \"A formal semantics of SQL queries, its validation, and applications (2017)\")**: A formal semantics of SQL queries, its validation, and applications (Its Validation, and Applications, PVLDB 11(1) 2017). Formal semantics of basic SQL with subqueries, bags, set operations and NULLs (3-valued logic). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/guagliardo2017semantics \"Summary\") · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3151113.3151116)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/theory-null\">theory-null</a></span>", "paper:harris2013sparql": "- **[SPARQL 1.1 Query Language](#/papers/harris2013sparql \"SPARQL 1.1 Query Language (2013)\")** (W3C Recommendation 21 March 2013 (a standard, not a paper; editors Harris and Seaborne)). The W3C standard that \"defines the syntax and semantics of the SPARQL query language for RDF\" (abstract): graph pattern matching with optional parts, alternatives, negation, property paths, aggregates, subqueries and four query forms (§5–§16). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/harris2013sparql \"Summary\") · [Paper](http://www.w3.org/TR/2013/REC-sparql11-query-20130321/)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sparql\">dsl-sparql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nondet-semantics\">nondet-semantics</a></span>", "paper:haynes2024geqo": "- **[GEqO](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2401.01280 \"GEqO: ML-Accelerated Semantic Equivalence Detection (2023)\")**: ML-Accelerated Semantic Equivalence Detection (PACMMOD 1(4) (SIGMOD 2024), 2023). Detects semantically equivalent SPJ subexpressions, aiming at cloud-scale workloads, for computation reuse; evaluated only on synthetic TPC-H/TPC-DS workloads (§7). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/haynes2024geqo \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2401.01280) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.01280) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3626710)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/judge\">judge</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a></span>", "paper:hu2024qrhint": "- **[Qr-Hint](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2404.04352 \"Qr-Hint: Actionable Hints Towards Correcting Wrong SQL Queries (2024)\")**: Actionable Hints Towards Correcting Wrong SQL Queries (SIGMOD 2024 (PACMMOD)). Given a correct target query and a wrong working query, gives step-by-step repairs (repair sites and fixes), clause by clause from FROM to SELECT, that the authors claim provably lead to a query equivalent to the target (abstract; §1; Theorem 3.1). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/hu2024qrhint \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2404.04352) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.04352) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3654995) · [Code](https://github.com/yihaoh/qr-hint)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/prove\">prove</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/smt\">smt</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a></span>", "paper:huang2026spa": "- **[SPA](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.08620 \"SPA: A SQL-Plan-Aware Reinforcement Learning Framework for Query Rewriting with LLMs (2026)\")**: A SQL-Plan-Aware Reinforcement Learning Framework for Query Rewriting with LLMs (preprint 2026). GRPO-trained rewriter with gated rewards: syntax, equivalence, plan change, measured latency. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/huang2026spa \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.08620) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08620)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite\">rewrite</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rlvr\">rlvr</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a></span>", "paper:ioannidis1995bag": "- **[Containment of conjunctive queries: beyond relations as sets](#/papers/ioannidis1995bag \"Containment of conjunctive queries: beyond relations as sets (1995)\")** (TODS 20(3) 1995). Containment under \"label systems\" (sets, bags and more), with homomorphism-type characterizations. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/ioannidis1995bag \"Summary\") · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/211414.211419)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/theory-bag\">theory-bag</a></span>", "paper:jayram2006inequalities": "- **[The containment problem for &lt;bi&gt;Real&lt;/bi&gt; conjunctive queries with inequalities](#/papers/jayram2006inequalities \"The containment problem for &lt;bi&gt;Real&lt;/bi&gt; conjunctive queries with inequalities (2006)\")** (PODS 2006). Bag containment of CQs with ≠ is undecidable, by reduction from Hilbert's tenth problem. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/jayram2006inequalities \"Summary\") · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/1142351.1142363)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/theory-bag\">theory-bag</a></span>", "paper:jiang2025grewriter": "- **[GRewriter](#/papers/jiang2025grewriter \"GRewriter: Practical Query Rewriting with Automatic Rule Set Expansion in GaussDB (2025)\")**: Practical Query Rewriting with Automatic Rule Set Expansion in GaussDB (PVLDB 18(12) 2025). Huawei GaussDB's rewriter: rules in G-DSL found by a WeTune-derived enumerator. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/jiang2025grewriter \"Summary\") · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3750601.3750622)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/qo\">qo</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/pairgen-apply\">pairgen-apply</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite-classic\">rewrite-classic</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-discover\">rules-discover</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-lib\">rules-lib</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-verify\">rules-verify</a></span>", "paper:jiang2025sqlgovernor": "- **[SQLGovernor (Tencent)](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2509.08575 \"SQLGovernor: An LLM-powered SQL Toolkit for Real World Application (2025)\")**: SQLGovernor: An LLM-powered SQL Toolkit for Real World Application (preprint, 2025). Toolkit for SQL syntax correction, query rewriting, query modification and equivalence verification, with LLM-generated, expert-validated rules (abstract; §4.3). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/jiang2025sqlgovernor \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2509.08575) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.08575) · [Code](https://github.com/TencentBigData-SiriusAI/SQLGovernor)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/judge\">judge</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite\">rewrite</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-eval\">nl2dsl-eval</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-discover\">rules-discover</a></span>", "paper:konstantinidis2026diophantus": "- **[Attacking Diophantus: Special Cases of Bag Containment](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.30956 \"Attacking Diophantus: Special Cases of Bag Containment (2026)\")** (preprint Sep 2026). A unified framework that decides bag-set containment of join-uniform CQs into arbitrary CQs, subsuming the projection-free and join-on-free cases; bag-bag stays open for join-uniform queries (§1, PDF p. 3; Thm. 6.2). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/konstantinidis2026diophantus \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.30956) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30956)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/theory-bag\">theory-bag</a></span>", "paper:lee2026mssqlrewrite": "- **[Verified LLM-Based Query Rewriting for Microsoft SQL Server](https://www.vldb.org/pvldb/vol19/p4594-narasayya.pdf \"Verified LLM-Based Query Rewriting for Microsoft SQL Server (2026)\")** (PVLDB 19(12) 2026 (demo)). SSMS extension: an anytime loop of LLM rewrite, QO-Verify, then timed execution. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/lee2026mssqlrewrite \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://www.vldb.org/pvldb/vol19/p4594-narasayya.pdf) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3827998.3828074)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite\">rewrite</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/scaling\">scaling</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/pairgen-check\">pairgen-check</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/prove-memo\">prove-memo</a></span>", "paper:li2024llmr2": "- **[LLM-R2](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2404.12872 \"LLM-R2: A Large Language Model Enhanced Rule-Based Rewrite System for Boosting Query Efficiency (2024)\")**: A Large Language Model Enhanced Rule-Based Rewrite System for Boosting Query Efficiency (PVLDB 18(1), 2024). The LLM selects which trusted Calcite rules to apply. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/li2024llmr2 \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2404.12872) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12872) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3696435.3696440) · [Code](https://github.com/DAMO-NLP-SG/LLM-R2)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite\">rewrite</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/pairgen-apply\">pairgen-apply</a></span>", "paper:li2025alphasql": "- **[Alpha-SQL](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2502.17248 \"Alpha-SQL: Zero-Shot Text-to-SQL using Monte Carlo Tree Search (2025)\")**: Zero-Shot Text-to-SQL using Monte Carlo Tree Search (ICML 2025). Zero-shot text-to-SQL by MCTS over seven fixed SQL-construction actions (Table 1), each carried out by an LLM prompt (§4.1). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/li2025alphasql \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2502.17248) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17248) · [Code](https://github.com/HKUSTDial/Alpha-SQL)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/scaling\">scaling</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-select\">nl2dsl-select</a></span>", "paper:li2026laser": "- **[LASER](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.06804 \"LASER: A Data-Centric Method for Low-Cost and Efficient SQL Rewriting based on SQL-GRPO (2026)\")**: A Data-Centric Method for Low-Cost and Efficient SQL Rewriting based on SQL-GRPO (preprint 2026). Small-model rewriter trained with SQL-GRPO on SQL-MCTS, a corpus of complex slow queries. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/li2026laser \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.06804) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06804)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite\">rewrite</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rlvr\">rlvr</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a></span>", "paper:liu2024genrewrite": "- **[GenRewrite](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2403.09060 \"GenRewrite: Query Rewriting via Large Language Models (2026)\")**: Query Rewriting via Large Language Models (PACMMOD 4(1) / SIGMOD 2026). LLM rewriting beyond fixed rules, with Natural Language Rewrite Rules (NLR2s) as hints and transferable knowledge. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/liu2024genrewrite \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2403.09060) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09060) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3786684)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/hacking\">hacking</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite\">rewrite</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/scaling\">scaling</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-discover\">rules-discover</a></span>", "paper:liu2026mira": "- **[MIRA (Memory-Item Reuse and Adaptation)](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.06950 \"MIRA: Evidence-Verified Repair Memory for Text-to-SQL Correction (2026)\")**: MIRA: Evidence-Verified Repair Memory for Text-to-SQL Correction (preprint 2026). A corrector for SQL from a text-to-SQL agent treated as a black box (§2.1) that reuses past confirmed corrections on the same database without weight updates: it splits each past correction into separate repair memory items, retrieves items for a new query, activates only those the question, the SQL and database observations support, and adapts them to the current SQL (abstract; §3.1). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/liu2026mira \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.06950) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06950)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/judge\">judge</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl\">nl2dsl</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/promptopt-context\">promptopt-context</a></span>", "paper:liu2026whichoracle": "- **[Certified Against Which Oracle?](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.25938 \"Certified Against Which Oracle? Execution Labels Set the Reported Risk of Conformal Abstention for Text-to-SQL (2026)\")**: Certified Against Which Oracle? Execution Labels Set the Reported Risk of Conformal Abstention for Text-to-SQL (preprint 2026). A preregistered intervention on a conformal abstention certificate for text-to-SQL (self-consistency over execution-equivalence classes, split-half conformal risk control): the clusters and the calibration labels come either from the benchmark's shipped database or from the distilled test suite of [Semantic Evaluation for Text-to-SQL…](#/papers/zhong2020testsuite \"Semantic Evaluation for Text-to-SQL with Distilled Test Suites (2020)\") (§1; §3.1–3.3), on Spider-Realistic with SQL-specialist checkpoints (§3.4). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/liu2026whichoracle \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.25938) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25938) · [Code](https://github.com/yahiko-l/text-to-sql-oracle-certificates)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/labels\">labels</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/scaling\">scaling</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/study\">study</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-data\">nl2dsl-data</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-eval\">nl2dsl-eval</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-select\">nl2dsl-select</a></span>", "paper:mang2025oracles": "- **[Argus](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2510.06663 \"Automated Discovery of Test Oracles for Database Management Systems Using LLMs (2025)\")**: Automated Discovery of Test Oracles for Database Management Systems Using LLMs (SIGMOD (PACMMOD), 2025). LLMs discover DBMS test oracles as Constrained Abstract Queries (equivalent-query skeletons). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/mang2025oracles \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2510.06663) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06663) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3802017) · [Code](https://github.com/joyemang33/Argus)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/hacking\">hacking</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-assist\">llm-assist</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nondet-eval\">nondet-eval</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/pairgen-check\">pairgen-check</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-discover\">rules-discover</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-verify\">rules-verify</a></span>", "paper:marcinkowski2025cqucq": "- **[Bag Semantics Query Containment: The CQ vs. UCQ Case and Other Stories](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2503.07219 \"Bag Semantics Query Containment: The CQ vs. UCQ Case and Other Stories (2025)\")** (PACMMOD 2025). Turns a UCQ into a CQ so containment questions transfer. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/marcinkowski2025cqucq \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2503.07219) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07219) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3767711)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/theory-bag\">theory-bag</a></span>", "paper:marcinkowski2025smallsteps": "- **[Bag Semantics Conjunctive Query Containment. (2024)\")** (PACMMOD 2(2) (PODS 2024)). Undecidability for small generalizations of bag CQ containment. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/marcinkowski2025smallsteps \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2503.18003) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18003) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3651604)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/theory-bag\">theory-bag</a></span>", "paper:mohammadi2026cypherlens": "- **[CypherLens: An Interactive Demo for Evaluating and Diagnosing NL-to-Cypher Systems](https://www.vldb.org/pvldb/vol19/p4558-mohammadi.pdf \"CypherLens: An Interactive Demo for Evaluating and Diagnosing NL-to-Cypher Systems (2026)\")** (PVLDB 19(12) 2026, demo paper). A platform for evaluating NL-to-Cypher systems (English questions to Cypher, a graph-database query language) beyond execution accuracy: it runs generated and gold queries on Neo4j, aligns their result columns, scores Exact Match, Soft Match and Dice, and has LLM agents label query types and diagnose errors against a Cypher-specific error taxonomy (abstract; §2, PDF pp. 2–3). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/mohammadi2026cypherlens \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://www.vldb.org/pvldb/vol19/p4558-mohammadi.pdf) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3827998.3828065) · [Code](https://github.com/YasmeanMohammadi/cypherLenz)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/judge\">judge</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/labels\">labels</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-graph\">dsl-graph</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-eval\">nl2dsl-eval</a></span>", "paper:narasayya2026qoverify": "- **[QO-Verify](https://www.vldb.org/cidrdb/papers/2026/p33-narasayya.pdf \"Leveraging Query Optimizers to Verify the Soundness of LLM-based Query Rewrites for Real-World Workloads, and More! (2026)\")**: Leveraging Query Optimizers to Verify the Soundness of LLM-based Query Rewrites for Real-World Workloads, and More! (CIDR 2026). Verifies an LLM rewrite by finding a common expression in the optimizer *memos* of original and rewrite. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/narasayya2026qoverify \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://www.vldb.org/cidrdb/papers/2026/p33-narasayya.pdf)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/qo\">qo</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite\">rewrite</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/scaling\">scaling</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/study\">study</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/pairgen-check\">pairgen-check</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/prove-memo\">prove-memo</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-discover\">rules-discover</a></span>", "paper:negri1991semantics": "- **[Formal semantics of SQL queries](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/111197.111212 \"Formal semantics of SQL queries (1991)\")** (TODS 16(3) 1991). Defines SQL query semantics by translation into an Extended Three-Valued tuple Predicate Calculus (§1–§3). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/negri1991semantics \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/111197.111212) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/111197.111212)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/theory-null\">theory-null</a></span>", "paper:neumann2015unnesting": "- **[Unnesting Arbitrary Queries](#/papers/neumann2015unnesting \"Unnesting Arbitrary Queries (2015)\")** (BTW 2015). Claims to unnest arbitrary correlated subqueries (\"the dependent join can be eliminated from any query\", §3.2) by pushing dependent joins down until they become regular joins. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/neumann2015unnesting \"Summary\") · [Paper](https://dl.gi.de/handle/20.500.12116/2418)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/qo\">qo</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite-classic\">rewrite-classic</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-lib\">rules-lib</a></span>", "paper:niu2026text2gql": "- **[Adaptive Text2GQL](https://aclanthology.org/2026.acl-long.804.pdf, \"Adaptive Text2GQL: Integrating Structural Twig Linking and Evolutionary In-Context Learning (2026)\")**: Integrating Structural Twig Linking and Evolutionary In-Context Learning (ACL 2026 (Volume 1: Long Papers; first-page footer, PDF p. 1)). A training-free pipeline that writes graph-database queries in Cypher, Gremlin and SPARQL from English questions: schema-filled query templates (\"twigs\") retrieved as hints, a few-shot example store that starts empty and grows during inference, and a correction agent that checks a query against regex rules and then runs it on the database, retrying with the error (abstract; §3–4, PDF pp. 1, 3–6). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/niu2026text2gql \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://aclanthology.org/2026.acl-long.804.pdf,) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2026.acl-long.804)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl\">nl2dsl</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-graph\">dsl-graph</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sparql\">dsl-sparql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/promptopt-context\">promptopt-context</a></span>", "paper:pan2026rulescript": "- **[RuleScript](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.05536 \"An Extensible and Verifiable Language for Query Rewrite Rules (2026)\")**: An Extensible and Verifiable Language for Query Rewrite Rules (preprint May 2026). An engine-independent DSL for query rewrite rules, with matching and transformation phases. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/pan2026rulescript \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.05536) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05536)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/smt\">smt</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/pairgen-apply\">pairgen-apply</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite-classic\">rewrite-classic</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-lib\">rules-lib</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-verify\">rules-verify</a></span>", "paper:pedrozo2026sqlzero": "- **[SQL-Zero](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.04697 \"SQL-Zero: Self-Evolving Text-to-SQL (2026)\")**: Self-Evolving Text-to-SQL (preprint 2026). Challenger–solver self-play with GRPO and zero annotated pairs. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/pedrozo2026sqlzero \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.04697) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04697) · [Code](https://anonymous.4open.science/r/sql-zero-artifact-478E)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rlvr\">rlvr</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-data\">nl2dsl-data</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nondet-eval\">nondet-eval</a></span>", "paper:pirahesh1992starburst": "- **[Starburst query rewrite](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/130283.130294 \"Extensible/Rule Based Query Rewrite Optimization in Starburst (1992)\")**: Extensible/Rule Based Query Rewrite Optimization in Starburst (SIGMOD 1992). An extensible rule engine that rewrites queries in the Query Graph Model (QGM) before plan optimization. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/pirahesh1992starburst \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/130283.130294) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/130283.130294)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/qo\">qo</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite-classic\">rewrite-classic</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-lib\">rules-lib</a></span>", "paper:pourreza2023evaluating": "- **[Evaluating Cross-Domain Text-to-SQL Models and Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2310.18538 \"Evaluating Cross-Domain Text-to-SQL Models and Benchmarks (2023)\")** (EMNLP 2023). Re-judges two top Spider models by hand on the dev questions both failed (§6.2) and rewrites Spider, Spider-DK and BIRD reference queries to return all tied rows (§6.1; not all rewrites keep the answer). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/pourreza2023evaluating \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2310.18538) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.18538) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2023.emnlp-main.99)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/study\">study</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-data\">nl2dsl-data</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-eval\">nl2dsl-eval</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nondet-eval\">nondet-eval</a></span>", "paper:renggli2025text2sql": "- **[Fundamental Challenges in Evaluating Text2SQL Solutions and Detecting Their Limitations](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2501.18197 \"Fundamental Challenges in Evaluating Text2SQL Solutions and Detecting Their Limitations (2025)\")** (preprint 2025). A taxonomy of text-to-SQL limitations that cause prediction and evaluation errors (abstract). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/renggli2025text2sql \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2501.18197) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18197)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/study\">study</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/survey\">survey</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-data\">nl2dsl-data</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-eval\">nl2dsl-eval</a></span>", "paper:ricciotti2020nulls": "- **[A Formalization of SQL with Nulls](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2003.11331 \"A Formalization of SQL with Nulls (2022)\")** (J. Autom. Reasoning 66(4) 2022). A Coq mechanization of SQL semantics with set and bag operations, lateral joins, nested subqueries and NULLs, with key metatheoretic properties validated (abstract). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/ricciotti2020nulls \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2003.11331) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2003.11331) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10817-022-09632-4) · [Code](https://github.com/wricciot/nullSQL)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/itp\">itp</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a></span>", "paper:sheng2025cscsql": "- **[CSC-SQL](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2505.13271 \"CSC-SQL: Corrective Self-Consistency in Text-to-SQL via Reinforcement Learning (2025)\")**: Corrective Self-Consistency in Text-to-SQL via Reinforcement Learning (Findings of IJCNLP-AACL 2025). Takes the two most frequent sampled SQL outputs and has a merge-revision model correct them. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/sheng2025cscsql \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2505.13271) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13271) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.findings-ijcnlp.91) · [Code](https://github.com/CycloneBoy/csc_sql)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rlvr\">rlvr</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/scaling\">scaling</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-data\">nl2dsl-data</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-select\">nl2dsl-select</a></span>", "paper:slivinskas2001foundation": "- **[A Foundation for Conventional and Temporal Query Optimization Addressing Duplicates and Ordering](#/papers/slivinskas2001foundation \"A Foundation for Conventional and Temporal Query Optimization Addressing Duplicates and Ordering (2001)\")** (IEEE TKDE 13(1) 2001). An algebra over lists, so it keeps duplicates and order, with temporal operations, and six kinds of equivalence: list, multiset and set, plus their snapshot forms (§3, §4). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/slivinskas2001foundation \"Summary\") · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1109/69.908979)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/qo\">qo</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nondet-semantics\">nondet-semantics</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite-classic\">rewrite-classic</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-lib\">rules-lib</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/theory-bag\">theory-bag</a></span>", "paper:song2025quite": "- **[QUITE](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2506.07675 \"QUITE: A Query Rewrite System Beyond Rules with LLM Agents (2025)\")**: A Query Rewrite System Beyond Rules with LLM Agents (preprint, 2025). FSM-driven multi-agent LLM rewriter with database feedback. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/song2025quite \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2506.07675) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07675) · [Code](https://github.com/Yuyang-Song/QUITE)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/judge\">judge</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite\">rewrite</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a></span>", "paper:stockmeyer1973word": "- **[Word Problems Requiring Exponential Time](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/800125.804029, \"Word Problems Requiring Exponential Time (Preliminary Report) (1973)\")**: Word Problems Requiring Exponential Time (Preliminary Report) (Preliminary Report (Stockmeyer and Meyer), STOC 1973). Pins down the inherent complexity of deciding whether two regular-like expressions (regular expressions over a set of letters, optionally with squaring or complement) describe different sets of words, and of similar word problems for integer expressions, quantified Boolean formulas and the first-order theory of equality, mostly as completeness under log-space or log-linear reductions (§2, table on PDF p. 3). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/stockmeyer1973word \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/800125.804029,) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/800125.804029)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/theory\">theory</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-regex\">dsl-regex</a></span>", "paper:sun2024rbot": "- **[R-Bot](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2412.01661 \"R-Bot: An LLM-based Query Rewrite System (2025)\")**: An LLM-based Query Rewrite System (PVLDB 18, 2025). LLM rewriting guided by retrieved rewrite evidence. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/sun2024rbot \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2412.01661) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01661) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3750601.3750625) · [Code](https://github.com/curtis-sun/LLM4Rewrite)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite\">rewrite</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/pairgen-apply\">pairgen-apply</a></span>", "paper:suresh2026grounding": "- **[Grounding Agent Memory: Environment-Probing Curation for Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.11060 \"Grounding Agent Memory: Environment-Probing Curation for Enterprise Agents (2026)\")** (preprint 2026). After each task a separate curator agent writes the agent's long-term memory from the trajectory and its grade (§3.2); this paper gives that curator read-only environment tools to check, narrow or refresh a candidate memory before committing it (\"propose–probe–commit\", §3.3). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/suresh2026grounding \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.11060) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11060)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/llm\">llm</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/nl\">nl</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/promptopt-context\">promptopt-context</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/promptopt-feedback\">promptopt-feedback</a></span>", "paper:wang2017mediator": "- **[Mediator](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1710.07660 \"Verifying Equivalence of Database-Driven Applications (2017)\")**: Verifying Equivalence of Database-Driven Applications (PACMPL 2(POPL) 2018 (POPL 2018), 2017). Verifies equivalence (and refinement) of two database-driven programs over different schemas, as in schema refactoring (abstract; refinement, §1). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/wang2017mediator \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1710.07660) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/1710.07660) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3158144)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/prove\">prove</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/smt\">smt</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a></span>", "paper:wang2022wetune": "- **[WeTune](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/3514221.3526125 \"WeTune: Automatic Discovery and Verification of Query Rewrite Rules (2022)\")**: Automatic Discovery and Verification of Query Rewrite Rules (SIGMOD 2022). Discovers rewrite rules by enumerating small plan-template pairs and their enabling constraints. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/wang2022wetune \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/3514221.3526125) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3514221.3526125) · [Code](https://github.com/WeTune/WeTune-code)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/prove\">prove</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/smt\">smt</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/pairgen-apply\">pairgen-apply</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite-classic\">rewrite-classic</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-discover\">rules-discover</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/rules-verify\">rules-verify</a></span>", "paper:wang2024qed": "- **[QED](https://www.vldb.org/pvldb/vol17/p3602-wang.pdf \"QED: A Powerful Query Equivalence Decider for SQL (2024)\")**: A Powerful Query Equivalence Decider for SQL (PVLDB 17(11) 2024). Q-expressions (semiring semantics), with SMT used in normalization and unification; NULLs, keys and foreign keys modelled. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/wang2024qed \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://www.vldb.org/pvldb/vol17/p3602-wang.pdf) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.14778/3681954.3682024)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/prove\">prove</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/smt\">smt</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a></span>", "paper:wang2026crystallization": "- **[Measuring Crystallization in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.07213 \"From Test-Time Scaling to Reusable Memory: Measuring Crystallization in Text-to-SQL (2026)\")**: From Test-Time Scaling to Reusable Memory: Measuring Crystallization in Text-to-SQL (preprint 2026). Measures the later value of stored, verified repair episodes: replay, cross-question retention, and held-out transfer on the same database, with the single-shot solver held fixed (abstract). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/wang2026crystallization \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.07213) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07213) · [Code](https://github.com/ai-jiaqian/text-to-sql-memory-crystallization)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/hacking\">hacking</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/scaling\">scaling</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/study\">study</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-select\">nl2dsl-select</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/promptopt-context\">promptopt-context</a></span>", "paper:wretblad2024noise": "- **[Understanding the Effects of Noise in Text-to-SQL: An Examination of the BIRD-Bench Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2402.12243 \"Understanding the Effects of Noise in Text-to-SQL: An Examination of the BIRD-Bench Benchmark (2024)\")** (ACL 2024 (short papers)). Two authors annotate every question and gold query of BIRD's financial-domain dev set (106 pairs) and 20 sampled pairs from each of four other domains for six kinds of noise, among them spelling or syntax errors, vague questions and incorrect gold SQL (§3.1–3.2; Tab. 1–2). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/wretblad2024noise \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2402.12243) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.12243) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2024.acl-short.34) · [Code](https://github.com/niklaswretblad/the-effects-of-noise-in-text-to-SQL)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/labels\">labels</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/study\">study</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-data\">nl2dsl-data</a></span>", "paper:xu2025e3rewrite": "- **[E3-Rewrite](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2508.09023 \"E3-Rewrite: Learning to Rewrite SQL for Executability, Equivalence, and Efficiency (2025)\")**: Learning to Rewrite SQL for Executability, Equivalence, and Efficiency (preprint, 2025). LLM rewriter trained with GRPO on executability, equivalence and cost rewards. <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/xu2025e3rewrite \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2508.09023) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09023)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/harness\">harness</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/judge\">judge</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rewrite\">rewrite</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rlvr\">rlvr</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a></span>", "paper:yan2025exspin": "- **[ExSPIN](https://www.mdpi.com/1099-4300/27/3/235/pdf \"ExSPIN: Explicit Feedback-Based Self-Play Fine-Tuning for Text-to-SQL Parsing (2025)\")**: Explicit Feedback-Based Self-Play Fine-Tuning for Text-to-SQL Parsing (*Entropy* 2025). Self-play fine-tuning (SPIN) for text-to-SQL, with the execution results of predicted SQL fed into the parameter update (abstract). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/yan2025exspin \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://www.mdpi.com/1099-4300/27/3/235/pdf) · [DOI](https://doi.org/10.3390/e27030235)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/method\">method</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rlvr\">rlvr</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-data\">nl2dsl-data</a></span>", "paper:yang2022balsa": "- **[Balsa](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2201.01441 \"Balsa: Learning a Query Optimizer Without Expert Demonstrations (2022)\")**: Learning a Query Optimizer Without Expert Demonstrations (SIGMOD 2022). Re-examines claims that RLVR learns almost as well from wrong annotations: the authors re-verify the \"incorrect\" math training set of [Spurious Rewards](#/papers/shao2025spurious \"Spurious Rewards: Rethinking Training Signals in RLVR (2025)\") with GPT-5 Pro, `math-verify`, a GPT-5 judge and manual checks, and remove the annotations that are in fact correct (abstract; §1; §3.3, Fig. 2). <span class=\"links\">[🔎](#/papers/zhu2026noisyrlvr \"Summary\") · [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2603.16140) · [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16140)</span> <span class=\"tags\"><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/hacking\">hacking</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/math\">math</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/rlvr\">rlvr</a><a class=\"tag\" href=\"#/tags/study\">study</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/dsl-sql\">dsl-sql</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/llm-gen\">llm-gen</a><a class=\"tag sub\" href=\"#/tags/nl2dsl-data\">nl2dsl-data</a></span>", "paper:zhu2026revisql": "- **[ReViSQL](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2603.20004 \"Human-Level Text-to-SQL via Reinforcement Learning on Verified Data, Without Pipeline Engineering (2026)\")**: Human-Level Text-to-SQL via Reinforcement Learning on Verified Data, Without Pipeline Engineering (preprint, 2026).
